- **stacked-prs**: A **stack** is a chain of PRs where each builds on the one below. The bottom PR's base is `main`, and each PR above takes the branch of the PR below as its base, so its diff shows only its own commits. - **stacked-prs**: A **stack** is a chain of open PRs, each based on the branch below it, with the bottom one based on `main`. GitHub shows the chain on every PR in it, and each PR's diff holds only its own commits, so the user reviews them one layer at a time. The `gh stack` extension (`github/gh-stack`) is installed; `gh stack &lt;command&gt; --help` is the full reference. ([StanleyGoldman/email-ops](https://github.com/StanleyGoldman/email-ops/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/stacked-prs.md))

- **stage-ordering**: Every stage is a set of greedy regexes over the whole text. There is no arbitration: whichever stage reaches a span of characters first claims it, and the stage that would have matched a *longer, more specific* span gets whatever debris is left. Debris is worse than no match at all, because it is usually still numeric and the numerals stage will happily read it as something. ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/standing-doc-gardener.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **standing-facts**: &gt; Moved verbatim from `AGENTS.md` by issue #3481 so that file stays a short router. Section numbers and headings are unchanged; a citation of "AGENTS.md §X" resolves here. Relative link targets were re-pointed from this folder; no rule text changed. ([popcre/shared-db](https://github.com/popcre/shared-db/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/standing-facts.md) · ★1)
- **STANDING-INVARIANTS**: ../../.pipeline/toolkit/claude-plugins/kampus-pipeline/agents/STANDING-INVARIANTS.md ([hueypov/kampus-pipeline](https://github.com/hueypov/kampus-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/STANDING-INVARIANTS.md))
- **STANDING-INVARIANTS**: A few entries in an agent definition's `## Standing invariants` section are **byte-identical** across two or more definitions. Those live here once instead of being re-carried per file. ([hueypov/kampus-pipeline](https://github.com/hueypov/kampus-pipeline/blob/HEAD/claude-plugins/kampus-pipeline/agents/STANDING-INVARIANTS.md))
- **standing-orders**: These are the rules every agent and person follows in the milestone workflow. They came out of the M7 run, where the operator gave 31 numbered orders during one night and day. This page keeps the ones that still need judgment, one rule each, with the reason and the incident behind it. A dispatch prompt names this page, and the [owner](owner.md) and [verifier](verifier.md) briefs link it. ([Munyon-Canyon/monaco](https://github.com/Munyon-Canyon/monaco/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/standing-orders.md))
- **standing-pool-cleanup**: Atomically removes a stale standing pool's index entry and metadata directory under the cross-session lock. ([bluminal/lumenai](https://github.com/bluminal/lumenai/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/standing-pool-cleanup.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **standing-pool-submitter**: Submits review tasks to a standing pool's task list and polls for the consolidated report envelope. ([bluminal/lumenai](https://github.com/bluminal/lumenai/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/standing-pool-submitter.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **standing-pr-reviewer**: Autonomous PR reviewer that runs on schedule to review open pull requests across managed repos. Checks code quality, security patterns, test coverage, and consistency with framework standards. ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/standing-pr-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **standing-release-scout**: Autonomous weekly check of whether the published npm package `ai-management` is behind `main`/`test` (latest npm version vs commits merged since its tag). When a release is warranted it PREPARES one — semver bump, CHANGELOG entry from conventional commits, release notes — on the `test` branch, then mails the operator to publish. Never runs `scripts/publish_release.sh` itself; publishing stays a human decision. ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/standing-release-scout.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **standing-rules**: The canonical block every `agents/&lt;profile&gt;/SOUL.md` must carry **verbatim**, per issue #873. One copy is kept here so a drift is a diff in one place instead of thirteen; each profile's SOUL still states it inline, because that is the text the agent actually runs under. ([asimov-agent/llgenie](https://github.com/asimov-agent/llgenie/blob/HEAD/agents/standing-rules.md) · ★1)
- **standing-security-auditor**: Scheduled security auditor that scans repos for common vulnerabilities, exposed secrets, insecure configurations, and OWASP compliance issues. ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/standing-security-auditor.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **standing-status-reporter**: Cross-repo status reporter that compiles a comprehensive daily status across all managed repos including git state, CI health, task queue, and recent activity. ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/standing-status-reporter.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **standing_actions**: Classify the complete actions that accepting an Agent's confirmation card would authorize. You do not grant authority; server code checks the user's registry, task constraints and independent condition evidence afterwards. ([nwverse/persona](https://github.com/nwverse/persona/blob/HEAD/server/internal/prompts/agents/standing_actions.md) · ★391)
- **standing_actions_input**: Node-verified task repository/directory: {{.Directory}} These directories belong to the task scope; different worktree paths in this list are verified repository aliases. ([nwverse/persona](https://github.com/nwverse/persona/blob/HEAD/server/internal/prompts/agents/standing_actions_input.md) · ★391)
- **standup**: The standup chat — board and git state at the start of a work session, and which ticket to pick up next. ([Laginho/PhysiSyst](https://github.com/Laginho/PhysiSyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/standup.md) · ★1)
- **standup**: Produce a short daily standup digest that holds work accountable — it reconciles what was committed to (in the last standup, meeting notes, and chat) against what actually shipped in the code. Use when asked for a "standup", "daily digest", "what did I ship yesterday", or "what's blocked". Read-only; safe to run unattended on a daily schedule. ([tracychen/project-routines](https://github.com/tracychen/project-routines/blob/HEAD/plugins/routines/agents/standup.md) · ★1)
